# Skills · R · part 24

894 entries, read-as-human-compressing to read-the-projects-origin-story. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **read-as-human-compressing**: Compress long contexts using the RAM (Read As Human) strategy: partition text into segments, score relevance against a query, fully retain high-relevance segments (close reading), and compress low-relevance segments into compact summaries (skimming). Use when: 'compress this long document for my query', 'summarize only the relevant parts', 'reduce context length before processing', 'skim this codebase for relevant sections', 'adaptive context compression', 'parallel segment relevance filtering'. ([ndpvt-web/arxiv-claude-skills](https://github.com/ndpvt-web/arxiv-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/read-as-human-compressing) · ★15)
- **read-as-markdown**: Convert binary document files (PDF, DOCX, EPUB) to cached Markdown for reading. Use when the user asks to "read a PDF", "convert docx to markdown", "extract text from a document", "show me this PDF as markdown", "read this paper", or mentions reading, viewing, or extracting text from .pdf, .docx, or .epub files. This skill is for READING documents as markdown — not for creating or editing them (use the pdf/docx skills for that). ([akaihola/skills-akaihola](https://github.com/akaihola/skills-akaihola/tree/HEAD/read-as-markdown) · ★2)
- **read-assembly-store**: Read and inspect .NET assembly stores from Android APK, AAB, or raw store files. Use this when users want to inspect, examine, or list assemblies in an APK, AAB, assembly store, or manifest file. ([review-bench/dotnet_android](https://github.com/review-bench/dotnet_android/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/read-assembly-store) · 2 copies)
- **read-assessment**: Reads a completed heritage significance assessment — uploaded, pasted, or produced earlier in the session — and offers analytical, interpretive and generative readings without writing new assessment stages. Use when the user says "read assessment", "analyze assessment", "review assessment", "קריאת הערכה", or uploads text that already contains CBSA stage outputs (values list, Nara Grid, significance statement) and chooses Read mode. ([InSites-Lab/insites](https://github.com/InSites-Lab/insites/tree/HEAD/system/claude/plugin/skills/read-assessment) · ★2)

- **read-guard**: Stop Claude Code from filling its context window with whole-file reads of huge files and log dumps. A PreToolUse hook that denies Read of files over ~800 lines / 80 KB without offset/limit, and `cat` / `type` / `Get-Content` of big files with nothing limiting the output (cd-aware), telling Claude to Grep and read a slice instead. Retrying the exact same call is allowed, so nothing is ever blocked for good. Use when the user hits usage limits fast, when claude-token-audit shows Read or Bash output dominating, or when they ask to make Claude Code cheaper. - **read-it-back**: Use right after a shell command or a script wrote or changed a file, and before running anything that depends on it - sed -i, perl -pi, a heredoc, echo or Set-Content redirection, python -c, node -e, a bulk find-and-replace, a codemod, a generator. Exit code 0 does not say the bytes landed, because the shell expands backticks, $ and backslashes inside the payload and a replacement that matches nothing still succeeds. Also use when writing such a command for a script or CI step, so it fails when nothing matched. Skip it after an edit tool that already fails on a missing match. ([efaimo-ai/efaimo-skills](https://github.com/efaimo-ai/efaimo-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/read-it-back) · 2 copies)
